Supplies **both definitions in full** and the **Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare (MHLW) 2008 severity score** (9 prognostic factors + CT grade). Independent risk factors: female sex, naïve papilla, surgically altered GI anatomy, procedure time after reaching the papilla, PD injection, **intraductal ultrasound (IDUS)**. ⚠ **The raw file is misnamed "Cotton Consensus"** — it is *not* the 1991 Cotton consensus, which it cites and tests against; tier-2 primary research, **no recommendations and no grades**
- [[azuma-2026-cryoprevention-pep]] — RCT (EUTOPIA, Am J Gastroenterol 2026;121:1224–1232): Local Postprocedural Cryoprevention for Post-ERCP Pancreatitis — Azuma, Kurita et al.; 8 Japanese hospitals, n=877 native papilla, single-blind. Ice-water irrigation of the papilla at end of ERCP: **PEP 3.2% vs 6.8% (P=0.02; RR 0.48, 0.26–0.89; NNT 28)**, benefit carried by mild PEP, no attributable adverse events. ⚠ Two limits bind transfer: **no arm received rectal NSAIDs** (Japanese insurance) and **the control arm received no irrigation at all**, so cooling cannot be separated from washing
- [[puente-2025-cirroxaban-rivaroxaban-cirrhosis]] — RCT (CIRROXABAN): rivaroxaban in cirrhosis — Puente et al.; feeds [[portal-vein-thrombosis]], [[portal-hypertension]], [[cirrhosis-hemostasis]]

### SAGES

- [[sages-2024-hiatal-hernia-surgery]] — Daly S, Kumar SS, Collings AT, et al. *SAGES guidelines for the surgical treatment of hiatal hernias.* Surg Endosc 2024;38:4765–75 — GRADE-based SAGES/EAES guideline on the **surgical management of type II, III and IV hiatal hernias**, complementing (not replacing) the society's 2013 document. **Four key questions, and the output is deliberately modest:** one conditional low-certainty recommendation (**perform a fundoplication during repair**), one explicit **decision not to make a recommendation at all** (**mesh**), and two **expert opinions** (repair of an asymptomatic hernia; conversion to Roux-en-Y gastric bypass for recurrence). **Certainty of evidence was low for every question.** Carries the type I–IV definitions, the effect sizes for fundoplication and for mesh, the criteria for establishing that a patient is genuinely asymptomatic, the watchful-waiting discussion built around unpredictable gastric volvulus, and the six circumstances favouring RYGB over redo repair. ⚠ **Sets no hernia-size threshold for repair and no BMI threshold for pre-repair weight loss** — both are named research gaps. ⚠ **Do not convert the mesh non-recommendation into a recommendation against mesh** — the panel considered exactly that and rejected it. **Cards: eligible** (tier-1 society guideline)
- [[sages-2021-gerd-surgery]] — SAGES 2021: Guidelines for Surgical Treatment of GERD — Slater, Dirks, McKinney et al. Surg Endoscopy 2021
- [[sages-2024-poem]] — SAGES 2024 Guideline Update: POEM for Achalasia — updated GRADE recommendations; POEM vs PD/LHM by achalasia subtype
- [[sages-2021-poem]] — SAGES 2021: Guidelines for the Use of POEM for Achalasia — Kohn et al. Surg Endosc 2021;35:1931–1948 (foundational; 4 GRADE recs: strong POEM>PD; POEM or LHM+fundoplication for subtypes I/II; POEM favored for subtype III; post-POEM GERD the key trade-off)

31. ~~`1-disease-scripts/colorectal-diseases/infections/shiga-toxin-producing-e-coli`~~ — **DONE 2026-10-12.** Built from [[idsa-2017-infectious-diarrhea]] (Recs 6/8/21/23/26/35/60) and [[acg-2016-acute-diarrhea]]; the STEC detail previously held by [[acute-diarrhea]] (HUS monitoring panel, sorbitol-MacConkey route) and the duplicated Rec 35 blurbs on five infection pages were **moved** to it, not copied

The row's own check was the blocker: **no ingested guideline printed the 7 types**, so the page waited for the primary paper, which was ingested this pass ([[lewis-1997-bristol-stool-form-scale]]). Built on it plus the four guidelines that act on the scale. Nine pages that each said in their own text that the types were missing now link here on first mention instead; **the thresholds stayed where they were** (IBS 25% subtype rule on [[irritable-bowel-syndrome]], Bristol ≥5 on [[hepatic-encephalopathy]] and [[lactulose]], Bristol <3 on [[chronic-constipation]]) — this page defines the types, not the bands, because Lewis & Heaton give no clinical cut-points. ⚠ Primary research, not a guideline → **no Anki cards**

Built on **8** ingested sources, not the 3 named — [[aga-2024-vasoactive-albumin-cirrhosis]] supplies the "albumin at LVP >5 L / lower doses ~4 g/L may suffice / no vasoconstrictors after LVP" set, [[aga-2022-aki-cirrhosis]] the competing weight-based 1 g/kg dose, [[acg-2020-hepatic-mesenteric-circulation]] the INR-8.7 safety datum and the creatinine >2.5 mg/dL platelet carve-out, [[aga-2021-cirrhosis-coagulation]] the low-bleeding-risk classification, and [[aasld-2024-aclf]] the decompensation-workup and respiratory-compromise indications. **This was a MOVE, not a copy** — the fluid panel and the LVP/PPCD block on [[ascites]] were reduced to pointers. ⚠ **One item in the original row could not be delivered: no ingested source prescribes a puncture site, needle, catheter or ultrasound technique**, so the page says so rather than supplying one — see *Needs a source*. The queue row's "20%–25% albumin" concentration is likewise not in the adult recommendations (AASLD prints 25% only in the **pediatric** statement) and was not asserted. *(Original row, for reference: core bedside procedure referenced across the hepatology pages with ample ingested sourcing: technique and site selection, diagnostic vs large-volume, the **>5 L → 20%–25% albumin 6–8 g per litre removed** rule and its smaller-volume exceptions (hypotension, renal insufficiency, electrolyte abnormalities), post-paracentesis circulatory dysfunction, **no prophylactic transfusion despite coagulopathy or thrombocytopenia**, and bedside inoculation into blood culture bottles — [[aasld-2021-ascites-sbp-hrs]], [[aga-2025-ascites-cirrhosis]], [[baveno-viii-2026-portal-hypertension]]. the fluid-interpretation thresholds (SAAG, cell count, SBP >250/mm³) stay on [[ascites]] and [[spontaneous-bacterial-peritonitis]] — this is a *procedure* page, link out rather than copy)*
35. ~~`5-meds/botulinum-toxin`~~ — **DONE 2026-10-19 (06:00 pass)** → [[botulinum-toxin]]. Built on **8** ingested sources, not the 2 named — [[asge-2020-achalasia]] supplies the pneumatic-dilation head-to-head, [[acg-2021-anorectal-disorders]] the anal-fissure / defecation-disorder / proctalgia stances, [[acg-2022-gastroparesis]] the Strong-Moderate recommendation *against* intrapyloric injection (AGA 2025 only suggests against), [[dallas-2025-flip-panometry]] the contractile-response predictor, [[aga-2024-poem-advances]] the nonachalasia spastic-disorder sequencing, [[aga-2024-esophageal-immunity-infection]] the cricopharyngeal use and [[rome-v-2026-dgbi]] the inability-to-belch (B3c) use. **This was a MOVE for the dose and decay data** — [[achalasia]] keeps the recommendation and the PD-vs-botulinum table (the treatment *choice* is a disease decision) and now points here for dose, technique and the relief-over-time table. **DES and EGJOO had no botulinum content of their own** beyond the AGA 2024 “exhaust less invasive options first” line, which is what the page carries; no dose, dilution, injection-site count or repeat interval exists in any ingested source for any indication other than achalasia, and the page says so rather than supplying one
36. ~~`5-meds/epinephrine`~~ — **DONE 2026-10-22 (06:00 pass)** → [[epinephrine]]. Built on **8** ingested sources, not the 2 named — [[asge-2014-lgib]] supplies the only injection *volumes* in the corpus (1-mL aliquots up to 2 mL, for diverticular bleeding) and its high-quality Rec 9, [[aga-2024-tailored-polypectomy]] the prophylaxis thresholds, [[asge-2020-endoscopic-removal]]/[[usmstf-2020-endoscopic-removal]] the intraprocedural-bleeding statement and the "uncertain in cold EMR" caveat, [[aga-2025-lifting-agents]] the lifting dilutions and the abdominal-pain harm, and [[aga-2023-alpha-gal-syndrome]] the autoinjector use. **This was a MOVE for the dose facts** — the concentration left [[endoscopic-hemostasis]] and the lifting dilutions left [[endoscopic-mucosal-resection]], each reduced to a pointer; both pages keep their strategy content. Also closed a real gap on [[acute-lower-gi-bleeding]], whose Step 6 listed clips, band ligation and bipolar coagulation from [[acg-2023-lgib]] with **no mention of epinephrine at all** — the older ASGE position is now surfaced there rather than silently dropped. ⚠ No ingested source gives an upper-GI volume, a cumulative-dose ceiling, or a systemic adverse-effect threshold, and the page says so
37. ~~`7-concepts/intraductal-ultrasound`~~ — **DONE 2026-10-23** → [[intraductal-ultrasound]]. **Re-routed to `4-advanced-gi-procedures/hepatobiliary-procedures/`**, not `7-concepts/` as the row proposed: IDUS is a specialized procedure performed at ERCP, and sits beside [[cholangioscopy]] in the same folder; `7-concepts/` is for pathophysiology, mechanisms and frameworks. Built on **4** ingested sources, not the 2 named — [[asge-2013-biliary-neoplasia]] supplies the hilar-staging accuracy (**95%–100%** combined with cholangioscopy, and more accurate than standard EUS) and the 3D-reconstruction point, and [[asge-2015-ampullary-duodenal-adenomas]] the ampullary numbers (tumour visualization **100%** vs EUS 59% vs CT 30%; tumour-diagnosis accuracy **88.9%** vs 56.3%, *P* = .05) together with the overstaging caveat and the **<1 cm ductal extension does not preclude endoscopic therapy** rule. **A MOVE, not a copy** — the accuracy figures left [[biliary-stricture]], which now carries only the one-line role statement and a pointer. ⚠ Surfaces a live disagreement between two same-year tier-1 guidelines: ASGE 2023 calls IDUS "more widely available now", ACG 2023 calls its availability **declining**. Neither recommends it, so no recommendation turns on it. ⚠ No ingested source grades the 6 malignant criteria, sets how many are needed, or provides the illustrating images — the page says so
38. ~~`3-general-gi-procedures/endoscopic-band-ligation`~~ — ✅ **created 2026-10-14 (cron)** → [[endoscopic-band-ligation]]. Also **`GI Guidelines/Other/Cotton Consensus.pdf` is not the Cotton consensus** — page 1 is Suzuki et al., *J Gastroenterol Hepatol Open* 2021 (doi `10.1002/jgh3.12687`), already ingested as [[suzuki-2021-pep-acute-pancreatitis-criteria]] (2026-10-22 audit). Also already covered: the four `AGA … (MSTF)` files → `usmstf-*` slugs; `ACG 2018 Management of Irritable Bowel Syndrome.pdf` (**misnamed** — it is Lacy 2021, = [[acg-2020-ibs]]); `ASGE 2025 Bowel Preparation Quality.pdf` → [[usmstf-2025-bowel-prep]]; `RCTs/Rosenbluth Urgent versus early ERCP….pdf` → [[jagtap-2026-urgent-vs-early-ercp-cholangitis]]; `RCTs/Prosty 2025 Prophylactic Antibiotics UGIB Cirrhosis.pdf` → [[albhaisi-2025-prophylactic-antibiotics-cirrhosis]] (slug uses a different author than first-author Prosty).
>
> **How to detect new arrivals — do not use `git status`.** `raw/`'s content subfolders are git-ignored (only `raw/assets/` is tracked), so newly synced PDFs never appear as untracked files, and the rsync rewrites mtimes so `find -newermt` is useless too. Match raw basenames against `wiki/sources/` titles **and then verify each hit by opening page 1** — basename matching alone produced both of the wrong counts above.

**NEW ARRIVAL 2026-10-16 — 1 file, and it was ingested the same day.** `rawcount.py` flagged `GI Guidelines/ASGE have=51 baseline=50`; `ls -lt` identified `ASGE 2026 Esophageal Gastric Varices.pdf` (synced 00:47). Ingested → [[asge-2026-esophageal-gastric-varices]], propagated to [[variceal-upper-gi-bleeding]], [[portal-hypertension]], [[nonselective-beta-blockers]] and [[antibiotic-prophylaxis-cirrhosis]]. **Baseline bumped to ASGE 51 / total 451 in the same pass**, per the standing rule that caught the 2026-10-08 sedation file out. ⚠ **It is the second tier-1 document published in 2026 to cover portal-hypertensive bleeding**, alongside [[baveno-viii-2026-portal-hypertension]] (accepted 30 July 2026 vs ASGE's 20 July 2026) — **same year, so neither supersedes the other by publication date.** Three live disagreements are carried side by side on [[variceal-upper-gi-bleeding]] rather than resolved: endoscopy timing (24 h vs 12 h), vasoactive duration (2–3 d vs 2–5 d), and the refractory bridge (either device vs covered stent preferred). **Do not silently collapse them to one position on a future pass.**

**Raw-folder hygiene noted (do not modify `raw/` — it is immutable):**
- `raw/GI Guidelines/AGA/` is **~40% redundant**: 33 lowercase shorthand copies + `AGA 2026 CPU Hemorrhoids (duplicate).pdf` + the ICI/Immune-Checkpoint pair (byte-identical, 2,420,918 bytes each) + 4 MSTF files duplicating `usmstf-*` — **35 redundant of 189** (2026-10-20 recount; the earlier "~39 of 175" predated the 2026-09-23 delivery of 14 files, and the 4 MSTF files are **not** redundant — they are the only copies of those USMSTF documents).
- `raw/GI Guidelines/Other/` holds **8 documents twice** under two naming conventions — `YYYY Name` vs `Name YYYY` (Chicago Classification v4.0, Lyon Consensus 2024, Dallas Consensus 2025, Padova Consensus 2025, San Diego Consensus 2025, Rome V 2026, Tokyo Guidelines 2018, EUS-PPG Delphi consensus). All ingested once. Singletons: ASCCP 2024, IANS 2024, IDSA 2017.
- `raw/GI RCTs/`: `Rosenbluth Urgent versus early ERCP….pdf` and `Urgent versus early ERCP….pdf` are the **same** Jagtap 2026 *Gut* RCT — 10 files, 9 distinct studies.
- **Misnamed:** `ACG/ACG 2018 Management of Irritable Bowel Syndrome.pdf` is actually Lacy BE et al., *Am J Gastroenterol* 2021;116:17–44. There is no 2018 ACG IBS monograph in `raw/`.
- The two `Aloysius 2026 DDW` `.png` files in `raw/GI Other Studies/` are the abstract and baseline table of one study, both covered by [[aloysius-2026-ercp-timing-septic-shock-cholangitis]].

### Needs a source
Topics and missing pieces that no file in `raw/` covers live on **[[needed-sources]]** — Nick's download list. Never write them from general knowledge.

## Syntheses

- [[uc-vs-crohns-comparison]] — UC vs. Crohn's: side-by-side comparison rebuilt strictly from ACG 2025 — colon-only inflammation clues, granulomas in only ≤33% (not pathognomonic), perianal CD 10–26% (not 30–40%), antineutrophil cytoplasmic antibody (ANCA)/anti-Saccharomyces cerevisiae antibody (ASCA) explicitly not for diagnosis, UC extent categories; classical teaching not in the guidelines listed separately as unsourced (2 sources)
